[1mcontent-terms height: the solver arithmetic[0m
  [32m✓[0m `rows * of` — pure arithmetic on the resolved dim, no theme facts needed at solve time

  [32m✓[0m `rows`+`of` tracks the TEN-FOOT ladder — `of` is a theme metric, and `metricScale` already scales it
  [32m✓[0m `lines` tracks the LIVE scale/offset a caller stamps on the dim (`layout_node.luau`'s job on the real pipeline)
  [32m✓[0m `rows`+`of` does NOT track the live accessibility offset — a theme metric never has, and that split is documented (ADR-0049), not a gap
  [32m✓[0m a control tree over BOTH shapes still measures every other node exactly as before (no cross-talk)

[1mruling 1: the degrade cascade descends only as far as it must[0m
  [32m✓[0m a row that FITS is untouched — the cascade never opens
  [32m✓[0m rung 1: it gives up its WRAP first, and keeps every character
  [32m✓[0m rung 2: a declared COMPACT form is spent before any character is
  [32m✓[0m rung 3: with nothing better to say it TRUNCATES, on one line, with the box intact
  [32m✓[0m the rungs are ORDERED: the same deficit truncates without a compact form and does not with one
  [32m✓[0m the compact rung SPARES A SIBLING: the label with something shorter to say gives it up
  [32m✓[0m an authored `minMax.min` is a CONTRACT, not a rung — the cascade never descends below it

[1mpreferred_text offset seam (LT-1/LT-3/LT-4)[0m

  [32m✓[0m an answer that raced a preference step is DISCARDED — the A->B->A double-step cannot poison the cache

  [32m✓[0m a FAILED lookup keeps the fallback and does not retry in a loop
  [32m✓[0m garbage answers are refused: NaN and negative offsets keep the fallback
  [32m✓[0m an answer past the ceiling is CLAMPED and applied, never traded for a smaller fallback
  [32m✓[0m an unknown future enum value starts from the LARGEST known fallback (over-reserve, never clip)
  [32m✓[0m dispose drops late answers on the floor

[1msortedEntries: the hazard it exists for[0m
  [32m✓[0m THE NEGATIVE CONTROL — the same twelve pairs iterate in DIFFERENT orders
  [32m✓[0m the naive determinism check would PASS with pairs — repetition is the wrong axis
  [32m✓[0m at SIX keys three of the four constructions agree — which is why this file uses twelve
[1msortedEntries: what it guarantees[0m
  [32m✓[0m every construction of the same dictionary yields ONE order
  [32m✓[0m the values ride along, unchanged and not copied
  [32m✓[0m numeric keys order NUMERICALLY, not lexicographically
  [32m✓[0m mixed number and string keys get one total order, deterministically
  [32m✓[0m an empty dictionary is an empty array, and the input is never mutated
  [32m✓[0m each call returns a FRESH array — a caller may sort or splice it
[1msortedEntries: the comparator[0m
  [32m✓[0m a caller-supplied key comparator is honoured, and stays deterministic
  [32m✓[0m the comparator receives KEYS, which is what makes the guarantee unconditional
  [32m✓[0m a key type with no natural order is REFUSED at construction, naming the comparator
  [32m✓[0m ...and is accepted once the caller supplies one
  [32m✓[0m a non-table dictionary and a non-function comparator are refused BY NAME
[1msortedEntries: through UI.ForEach[0m
  [32m✓[0m a dictionary signal drives a keyed region in one order whatever built it
[1mengine styling: shadows and per-corner rounding (§17)[0m

  [32m✓[0m a token preset shadow resolves from the style's shadow tokens
  [32m✓[0m shadows never enter layout: identical rects with and without a shadow
  [32m✓[0m uniform corners emit the alias form; distinct corners emit the per-corner form
  [32m✓[0m corner radii resolve token names from the active style
  [32m✓[0m mixing the alias and per-corner radii in one spec is a build error

[1mexamples/05 word game — the pure scoring rules[0m
  [32m✓[0m scores a plain guess with no repeated letters
  [32m✓[0m a guess with a DOUBLED letter against a solution holding ONE spends the budget once
  [32m✓[0m a SOLUTION with a doubled letter pays a misplaced guess letter from what the exact match left
  [32m✓[0m BOTH doubled: two exact matches and one misplaced, all from a budget of two
  [32m✓[0m an EXACT match consumes the budget BEFORE an earlier misplaced copy of the same letter
  [32m✓[0m three copies against two: the excess is absent
  [32m✓[0m the whole solution scores correct
[1mexamples/05 word game — validation and the two word lists[0m
  [32m✓[0m refuses a guess shorter than five letters
  [32m✓[0m refuses a five-letter non-word
  [32m✓[0m accepts every solution AND the wider guess list
  [32m✓[0m the solution list is a SUBSET of the accepted list, and strictly smaller
[1mexamples/05 word game — the key ladder is monotonic[0m
  [32m✓[0m ranks correct above present above absent
  [32m✓[0m never downgrades on later, weaker evidence
  [32m✓[0m an unknown key takes whatever the first evidence says
  [32m✓[0m mergeKeys returns a NEW table and keeps the strongest per letter
  [32m✓[0m a later guess that re-tests a solved letter cannot downgrade it
[1mexamples/05 word game — the deterministic seed[0m
  [32m✓[0m the same seed always yields the same word
  [32m✓[0m consecutive seeds walk the solution list and wrap
  [32m✓[0m seed 1 is REACT, the word the tutorial text quotes
[1mexamples/05 word game — the state machine[0m
  [32m✓[0m starts on a visible six-by-five board with a caret in the first cell
  [32m✓[0m every tile paints a REAL tint — the plate is never invisible
  [32m✓[0m letters land in the ACTIVE row only
  [32m✓[0m a SHORT guess is refused with a message and does not consume a row
  [32m✓[0m an INVALID guess is refused with a different message and does not consume a row
  [32m✓[0m backspace deletes from the active row and stops at empty
  [32m✓[0m a scored row keeps its verdict AND its marks while the next row becomes active
  [32m✓[0m the duplicate-letter rule reaches the board, not just the scorer
  [32m✓[0m key state accumulates monotonically and never downgrades
  [32m✓[0m wins when the solution is submitted, and opens the results card
  [32m✓[0m loses after SIX accepted wrong guesses and reveals the word
  [32m✓[0m a refused guess does NOT count toward the six
  [32m✓[0m input is dead after the game ends, except restart
  [32m✓[0m restart is deterministic: the same seed replays the same word
  [32m✓[0m restart after a loss closes the results card and re-enables the keyboard
  [32m✓[0m the results card's Close button actually closes it
  [32m✓[0m the results card's New game button restarts from inside the card
  [32m✓[0m tears down to a registry baseline after dispose

[1m§2.6 distribute — the three flex distributions[0m
  [32m✓[0m spaceBetween puts the leftover in the gaps only (0 / 200 / 400)
  [32m✓[0m spaceEvenly gives every gap AND both ends the same share (50 / 200 / 350)
  [32m✓[0m spaceAround gives the ends a HALF share (33 / 199 / 366)

  [32m✓[0m a single child: spaceBetween pins it at the start, spaceAround centres it
  [32m✓[0m a vertical stack distributes along Y, not X

[1m§2.4 shrink — the inner distribution (`shrinkWeight`)[0m
  [32m✓[0m equal weights and equal bases split the deficit evenly
  [32m✓[0m the weight is MULTIPLIED BY THE BASIS, so the small child is not collapsed first
  [32m✓[0m `shrinkWeight = 0` (the default) never shrinks, whatever its siblings do
  [32m✓[0m a bigger weight gives up proportionally more
[1m§2.4 shrink — the outer sort (`layoutPriority`)[0m
  [32m✓[0m the lowest tier absorbs the whole deficit before the next tier gives a pixel
  [32m✓[0m a tier that runs out of room passes the rest UP to the next tier
  [32m✓[0m a NEGATIVE priority is the first to give way
[1m§2.4 shrink — the floor cascade: minMax.min, longest word, compact form, ellipsis, clip[0m
  [32m✓[0m `minMax.min` is a hard floor inside its own tier
  [32m✓[0m a text node stops at the width of its longest word while that is enough
  [32m✓[0m ...and DESCENDS past it, to an ellipsis, when the longest word is not enough
  [32m✓[0m ...and a declared COMPACT form is spent BEFORE any of the string is
  [32m✓[0m THE CLOSED SEAM: the row RESERVES the cross extent a squeezed text will need
  [32m✓[0m ...and the measure-side pass is INERT: the same row without a weight is unmoved
  [32m✓[0m ...and the REPORT moves with the RESERVATION: a `content` row fits what it shrank into
  [32m✓[0m ...and the shrunk text is RE-MEASURED at the reduced offer, so its box covers its wraps
  [32m✓[0m the measure MEMO tells the two offers apart — the cache key already covers the pass
  [32m✓[0m ...and the MEASURE-side pass is keyed by the memo too, at the same key
  [32m✓[0m with no floor left the row fits exactly and nothing overflows

  [32m✓[0m the overflow diagnostic names the priority order the pass tried
  [32m✓[0m a row that never opted in gets NO priority suffix (today's message, unchanged)

  [32m✓[0m ...and so does an ordinary reactive prop change, through refresh's incremental set
  [32m✓[0m a prop round trip that will not converge is a LOUD error, not a hang

[1mmotion classes (the vocabulary)[0m
  [32m✓[0m ships the four ratified shapes under framework-neutral names
  [32m✓[0m overshoot is earned: reward is the ONLY under-damped built-in

[1mspring solver (semi-implicit Euler)[0m
  [32m✓[0m a critically damped class never overshoots its target
  [32m✓[0m the one under-damped class overshoots (earned, and only there)
  [32m✓[0m setTarget touches neither value nor velocity (the interruptibility invariant)
  [32m✓[0m an interrupted flight carries its velocity: no brick wall against a cut twin
  [32m✓[0m a seeded release velocity produces a continuous first frame (no seam)
  [32m✓[0m clamps a frame spike and substeps it: no NaN, and a spike equals MAX_DT
  [32m✓[0m rejects non-finite input and keeps the last good state
  [32m✓[0m snap sets value and target and zeroes velocity (the reduced-motion primitive)
  [32m✓[0m settles within eps on BOTH value and velocity, and a coarser eps settles sooner
  [32m✓[0m a zero or negative dt advances nothing (a rewound clock is not motion)
[1mmotion clock: stepping and the active set[0m
  [32m✓[0m a value is a Readable the rest of the framework already knows how to bind
  [32m✓[0m derives dt from the injected now() when step() is called bare
  [32m✓[0m commits every value in ONE transaction per stepped frame
  [32m✓[0m settled values leave the active set: rest costs zero steps and zero writes
  [32m✓[0m lands EXACTLY on the target and fires onSettle exactly once per flight
  [32m✓[0m a mid-flight re-target continues from the current value and velocity
  [32m✓[0m a function target is re-read every step, so a moved target still lands
  [32m✓[0m a vanished live target freezes the aim on the last good read and settles
  [32m✓[0m stop abandons the aim where the value stands, and fires no arrival
  [32m✓[0m snap is placement, not arrival: value+target set, no settle event
  [32m✓[0m refuses inline class params at the call site
  [32m✓[0m refuses an unknown motion kind
[1mmotion clock: reduced motion (information parity)[0m
  [32m✓[0m a decorative value snaps to the target and fires the SAME settle event
  [32m✓[0m a velocity seed is inert under reduced motion (there is no seam to smooth)
  [32m✓[0m the policy is read LIVE: a flight in progress snaps on its next re-target
  [32m✓[0m an informational value KEEPS RUNNING under RM, quantized to 250 ms writes
[1mmotion clock: informational timers[0m
  [32m✓[0m depletes linearly and reaches its terminus on the exact wall-clock frame
  [32m✓[0m under RM it still reaches the terminus at the same moment, with stepped writes
  [32m✓[0m a DECORATIVE timer is over immediately under RM (declared, not defaulted)
  [32m✓[0m a zero-duration timer is already at its terminus and still announces it
  [32m✓[0m a timer has no target and no velocity to seed
[1mmotion clock: glides (a value RESAMPLED at a fixed cadence)[0m
  [32m✓[0m crosses the gap at CONSTANT velocity — the assertion a spring cannot pass
  [32m✓[0m re-aiming mid-flight restarts the ramp FROM WHERE IT IS — no teleport, no stall
  [32m✓[0m is INFORMATIONAL by default: under RM it keeps gliding, quantized, never snapping
  [32m✓[0m a zero duration is legal and lands immediately (a cadence that is one frame)
  [32m✓[0m refuses an unknown key and a negative duration (constitution §4)
[1mmotion clock: counters (the numeral a player reads)[0m
  [32m✓[0m publishes WHOLE numbers on the way, and lands exactly on the count
  [32m✓[0m NEVER overshoots the count, even on an under-damped class
  [32m✓[0m writes ONCE PER CHANGED NUMERAL, not once per frame
  [32m✓[0m re-aims mid-count without restarting, and lands on the LAST count asked for
  [32m✓[0m under reduced motion the FINAL COUNT is instant, and the settle event still fires
  [32m✓[0m a fractional target still lands on a numeral, and getTarget says which
  [32m✓[0m snap places the count with no arrival, and rest costs zero

  [32m✓[0m a malformed event is refused rather than delivered half-formed
[1mfeedback bus: subscriptions[0m
  [32m✓[0m unsubscribing stops delivery and leaves no slot behind
  [32m✓[0m a handler may unsubscribe itself from inside its own delivery
  [32m✓[0m a handler subscribing during a delivery does not receive that same event
[1mpresenter feedback: activate[0m
  [32m✓[0m fires exactly once per press, on the causal frame, after the effect
  [32m✓[0m a disabled control emits nothing — nothing happened
  [32m✓[0m handle.onFeedback sees only its own surface's events

  [32m✓[0m onModalPresented is the modal-cancels-drag hook, and it unsubscribes
[1mUI.sensoryFeedback: the declaration is ruled on at the call site[0m
  [32m✓[0m refuses an event outside the closed twelve, listing the vocabulary
  [32m✓[0m accepts every one of the twelve verbs
  [32m✓[0m refuses a trigger that is not a Signal/Memo — a constant never changes
  [32m✓[0m refuses an unknown spec key rather than dropping it
  [32m✓[0m refuses a structural region — it would mount no node to emit for
  [32m✓[0m returns a NEW frozen blueprint and leaves the original untouched
[1mUI.sensoryFeedback: what reaches the bus[0m
  [32m✓[0m emits the declared verb with the mounted path when the trigger changes
  [32m✓[0m fires once per change, and not at all for a write of the same value
  [32m✓[0m emits synchronously inside the write that moved the trigger
  [32m✓[0m stops emitting the moment the node unmounts
  [32m✓[0m composes: two declarations on one node both fire
  [32m✓[0m observes NOTHING when no sink is wired, and exactly one thing when one is
[1mUI.sensoryFeedback: through the real presenter[0m
  [32m✓[0m reaches presenter.onFeedback, carrying the surface it happened on
  [32m✓[0m is visible on handle.onFeedback, the per-surface filter
[1msensory profile: the three defaults are the product, pinned exactly[0m
  [32m✓[0m press is `contact` — one short, crisp tap when the action goes down
  [32m✓[0m release is `settle` — a lighter, rounder answer when the action completes
  [32m✓[0m select is `tick` — the smallest audible-to-the-hand step for a changed choice
  [32m✓[0m the three names are distinct, and PHASES lists exactly the three phases
  [32m✓[0m no default key authors an intensity below the documented trigger floor
  [32m✓[0m no default outlasts the overlap budget, and every key list rises in time from 0
  [32m✓[0m every mode names a real Enum.KeyInterpolationMode member
  [32m✓[0m the defaults are FROZEN all the way down — a consumer cannot edit the product
[1msensory profile: resolve() merges a partial over the defaults[0m
  [32m✓[0m nothing supplied is the defaults, value for value
  [32m✓[0m a game replaces ONE phase and keeps the other two
  [32m✓[0m a game swaps a phase for a PRESET without touching any control
  [32m✓[0m a game SILENCES one phase, and silence is a decision rather than an omission
  [32m✓[0m all three at once, in one call
  [32m✓[0m the result is frozen too, so a resolved profile cannot be edited behind the adapter
  [32m✓[0m an unknown phase key is an authoring error that names the three phases
  [32m✓[0m a Custom phase with NO keys is refused — it is the guaranteed silent no-op
  [32m✓[0m a spec whose `kind` is not one of the three is refused
  [32m✓[0m a preset naming something that is not a HapticEffectType is refused
  [32m✓[0m `Custom` may not be named as a PRESET — it is a silent no-op by construction
[1msensory profile: the preset FALLBACK, and the limitation it carries[0m
  [32m✓[0m names one preset per phase, and every one is a real HapticEffectType
  [32m✓[0m is TOTAL over the phases — a fourth phase would be a visible gap
  [32m✓[0m release and select SHARE UIHover, and that is the documented limitation
  [32m✓[0m fallbackFor(phase) hands back the phase's preset SPEC, ready to pool
[1msensory profile: key() is what makes pooling possible[0m
  [32m✓[0m two specs that would FEEL the same share a key; different ones do not
  [32m✓[0m the three default keys are distinct — three waveforms, three instances
  [32m✓[0m label() names a sensation for a reader — the waveform, or the preset, or nothing
[1msensory profile: it is ENGINE-FREE, which is why any of this is provable[0m
  [32m✓[0m requires nothing and names no engine global — in CODE, not in prose
[1mhaptics: default OFF means the engine is never touched[0m
  [32m✓[0m constructs nothing and plays nothing until it is switched on
  [32m✓[0m publishes no platform claim while it is off

[1mhaptics: the map is TOTAL over the twelve verbs[0m
  [32m✓[0m every verb in the taxonomy has an explicit entry — no silent drop
  [32m✓[0m the map invents no verb the taxonomy does not have
  [32m✓[0m exactly five verbs map to nothing, and they are the five decided

  [32m✓[0m `activate` names no preset on the bus — the two edges belong to two owners
  [32m✓[0m the two verbs the SELECT phase claims have NO down edge of their own
  [32m✓[0m `pressSpecFor` answers the four cases a control can be in
  [32m✓[0m `activationIsFelt` is the OTHER question, and the two are not the same
[1mhaptics: what an enabled adapter actually plays[0m
  [32m✓[0m plays one pooled effect per mapped verb, and reuses it forever
  [32m✓[0m the five silent verbs never play, however often they arrive
  [32m✓[0m a played effect carries the resolved enum value, never a bare Custom
[1mhaptics: `adjust` is rate-limited (a slider is not a buzzsaw)[0m
  [32m✓[0m coalesces a per-tick storm down to one play per interval
  [32m✓[0m the next tick after the interval plays again
  [32m✓[0m coalescing DROPS — a swallowed tick never arrives late as a phantom
  [32m✓[0m the rate limit is the SELECT PHASE's — discrete verbs are never swallowed
[1mhaptics: the capability probe is a LATTICE, never a boolean[0m
  [32m✓[0m touch is `unknown`, not `unsupported` — there is no probe for a phone
  [32m✓[0m the pre-first-gamepad state is `unknown` too
  [32m✓[0m a CONNECTED pad answering false is the one case that means `unsupported`
  [32m✓[0m a connected pad answering true is `supported`
  [32m✓[0m no input service at all is `unknown`, never a negative claim
  [32m✓[0m re-probes on hot-plug instead of caching the boot answer
  [32m✓[0m re-probes on LastInputTypeChanged as well
  [32m✓[0m disconnects its device listeners when switched off
[1mhaptics: the enum is resolved defensively, and never falls back[0m

[1mhaptics: a client without the class degrades, it does not crash[0m
  [32m✓[0m an uncreatable class is `absent`, with the engine's own words kept
  [32m✓[0m any other refusal is `blocked` — the two are not the same fact
  [32m✓[0m with NO seams injected at all it still never throws (this very process)
[1mhaptics: `activate` takes the PROPERTY route (the engine plays it)[0m
  [32m✓[0m assigns PressHapticEffect and NEVER calls Play
  [32m✓[0m leaves HoverHapticEffect alone — a felt cursor is nobody's request

[1mhaptics: an effect is DESTROYED, never merely dropped[0m

  [32m✓[0m dispose destroys the pool AND the press effect
  [32m✓[0m disposing an already-OFF adapter destroys nothing twice
[1mhaptics: a detach is LOCAL[0m

[1mhaptics: an `absent` class is asked exactly once[0m
  [32m✓[0m 50 events cost ONE construction attempt, not 50
  [32m✓[0m ...and `blocked` is NOT short-circuited — the two verdicts are not the same fact
[1mhaptics: lifetime[0m

[1mhaptics: the press phase is the ENGINE's, and it is the Facet waveform[0m

[1mhaptics: the release phase is the COMPLETED edge, and it is new[0m

[1mhaptics: press and completed-release in one instant collapse to one sensation[0m
  [32m✓[0m a non-pointer activation plays `settle` ONCE, and nothing else in that instant
  [32m✓[0m the collapse is ONE INSTANT wide — the next frame is a new gesture

  [32m✓[0m a POINTER press is NOT collapsed — its two moments are genuinely apart

  [32m✓[0m the two verbs share ONE limiter — a scrubbed picker cannot alternate past it

[1mthe cascade: nearest declaration wins, and costs nothing when absent[0m

  [32m✓[0m a NEARER declaration overrides a farther one

[1mbounded retry: the schedule[0m
  [32m✓[0m a failure retries up to `count` times and then reports failed
  [32m✓[0m a retry that succeeds resolves normally, and the failed attempt stays stale
  [32m✓[0m a spaced retry is invisible to the transport until its delay has elapsed
  [32m✓[0m releasing the last waiter cancels a retry that is still waiting out its delay
[1mbounded retry: the give-up is permanent for the session[0m
  [32m✓[0m a spent budget fails the key, and a later acquire does not start it over
  [32m✓[0m invalidate is the explicit reset
  [32m✓[0m the LEGACY retryAttempts option keeps its old promise: a failed key re-requests
  [32m✓[0m a per-acquire retry overrides the provider's policy for that key
[1mprovider construction is strict (F-12)[0m
  [32m✓[0m maxConcurrent = 0 is refused instead of silently wedging every request
  [32m✓[0m maxConcurrent must be a positive INTEGER
  [32m✓[0m cacheBudget must be a non-negative integer (0 is legal: cache nothing)
  [32m✓[0m a negative retry count or delay is reported, not silently discarded
  [32m✓[0m no opts at all, and an empty opts table, still build the documented defaults
[1mpreload: warming a declared set[0m
  [32m✓[0m preloads exactly the keys it was handed, under the same concurrency window
  [32m✓[0m a released preload skips work that has not started
  [32m✓[0m a warmed key is already ready when the view finally acquires it — no placeholder
  [32m✓[0m a live view keeps a preloaded request alive after the warm set is released
  [32m✓[0m preload never re-opens a cached or given-up key
  [32m✓[0m preload churn returns the provider's counters to baseline

[1minteraction tokens: promotion thresholds (SF-D3)[0m
  [32m✓[0m ships the ratified per-class values, inside their published ranges
  [32m✓[0m non-pointer classes carry ZERO: an armed drag has no travel to measure
  [32m✓[0m maps engine pointer types to classes, and an unknown type is a pointer
  [32m✓[0m promotion is a MAGNITUDE test, not two axis tests
  [32m✓[0m the same travel is a drag on a mouse and still a tap on a finger
  [32m✓[0m per-call overrides win, and absent keys still fall through to the token
  [32m✓[0m Table's reorder threshold is now the TOKEN, not a private copy
[1minteraction tokens: the responder priority bands (framework-gaps-phase2 item 41)[0m
  [32m✓[0m publishes the three ADR-0014 bands presenter.luau's own comment states
  [32m✓[0m matches presenter.luau's own literals — a drift here fails loudly instead of shipping quietly wrong
  [32m✓[0m is frozen — a consumer cannot mutate the shared band table
[1mdrag velocity: the rolling release window (SF-D2)[0m
  [32m✓[0m answers zero until it has two samples
  [32m✓[0m estimates first-vs-last across the retained window
  [32m✓[0m drops samples older than the window: a flick after a pause reads as a FLICK
  [32m✓[0m keeps TWO samples even when both are stale: a paused gesture answers a real zero
  [32m✓[0m a non-advancing clock is zero, never a division by zero
  [32m✓[0m refuses non-finite samples rather than poisoning the estimate
  [32m✓[0m reset clears the buffer (the read order contract: velocity THEN reset)
  [32m✓[0m honours a custom window and reports it
[1mautoscroll: band membership is the POINTER point[0m
  [32m✓[0m the middle of the viewport is in no band
  [32m✓[0m the top band arms upward and the bottom band arms downward
  [32m✓[0m a point outside the host horizontally is in no band
  [32m✓[0m bands never overlap: a host shorter than two bands splits at the midpoint
[1mautoscroll: dwell is what makes a flick-through safe[0m
  [32m✓[0m does not move before the dwell elapses
  [32m✓[0m jitter INSIDE the band accrues dwell; it never resets it
  [32m✓[0m a flick THROUGH the band never arms
  [32m✓[0m crossing to the other band restarts the dwell
  [32m✓[0m re-entering the band it just left re-pays the dwell
  [32m✓[0m justArmed is true on exactly ONE frame per arming
[1mautoscroll: speed is linear in penetration, behind a start ramp[0m
  [32m✓[0m deeper into the band is faster
  [32m✓[0m the penetration mapping is v = vMin + (vMax - vMin) * p
  [32m✓[0m the start ramp eases from zero over rampS, from the ARMING instant
  [32m✓[0m leaving while active decays to zero over exitEaseS instead of cutting
  [32m✓[0m a DIRECT band cross coasts the old velocity out instead of cutting it (D-11)
  [32m✓[0m arming supersedes a coast rather than adding to it
  [32m✓[0m leaving the band mid-coast keeps decaying instead of re-cutting

  [32m✓[0m a large offer stays on the richest arrangement (desktop)
  [32m✓[0m the choice follows the OFFER, not a viewport: the same width decides differently by height
  [32m✓[0m Q9: with twoLane withheld, an offer that fails threeLane falls to the column
  [32m✓[0m when NO candidate is legal the LAST one shows, flagged as the declared fallback
[1mADR-0023: step down before dropping, both in descending rank[0m
  [32m✓[0m a lane that overflows steps its LEAST important region down first
  [32m✓[0m squeezing further walks UP the rank order, one region at a time
  [32m✓[0m NOTHING is dropped while any region in the failing lane can still step down
  [32m✓[0m only then are regions dropped, still in descending rank
  [32m✓[0m a RANK TIE breaks by declaration order — the later-declared steps down first
  [32m✓[0m a region with ONE form cannot step down, so rank 1 keeps its plate
  [32m✓[0m a step-down that cannot relieve the failure is not taken (it would only cheapen the screen)
  [32m✓[0m a lane PINNED by a region that cannot narrow gives up nothing at all
  [32m✓[0m ...but two regions that can BOTH give way still do, one at a time
[1mADR-0023: floors are content, and absolute[0m
  [32m✓[0m a `lines` floor resolves through the live theme's type role, CEILED as the solve is
  [32m✓[0m a `targets` floor resolves through the theme's minimum touch target
  [32m✓[0m both stated takes the LARGER — a floor is a floor
  [32m✓[0m a bigger type scale raises the floor by itself, with no author involvement
  [32m✓[0m no declared floor means the minimum-viable form's own measure
  [32m✓[0m the FILL region never sinks below its floor — the lane is refused instead
[1mADR-0023: slack flows to fill; reserved holds; empty is absent[0m
  [32m✓[0m every pixel left over goes to the fill region, on both axes

  [32m✓[0m a reserved region is never dropped, however tight the offer
  [32m✓[0m a NON-reserved region whose content resolves to nothing is not mounted and costs no gap
  [32m✓[0m maxMeasure caps both axes and centres the result
[1mADR-0023 rule 9 (DV6-2): an all-empty lane COLLAPSES and gives up its width[0m

  [32m✓[0m dropped by rule 3 -> collapsed=true
  [32m✓[0m reserved, schedule OVER (the quiet round) -> collapsed=true

  [32m✓[0m the released width AND its lane gap go to the fill lane, to the pixel

  [32m✓[0m MID-SEQUENCE STABILITY survives the release: an ACTIVE reserve holds its box between pieces
  [32m✓[0m the dump carries `collapsed`, and every lane a reader already saw is unchanged
  [32m✓[0m releasing the FLAG is not enough: a form that paints unconditionally is never empty
  [32m✓[0m a `reserved` that is not a boolean is refused where it is written
